user

**Role & Objective**
Kamu adalah **Senior Android Engineer**. Kita masuk ke **Tahap 32.2**. Pengguna melaporkan bahwa setelah menyalin pengaturan WebView dari sweb-master, halamannya **masih blank putih**, NAMUN aktivitas jaringan (*network*) menunjukkan bahwa elemen web/JS/CSS berhasil diunduh.
Ini berarti kegagalannya ada pada *rendering engine*, *Compose layout*, atau interseptor custom kita. Tugasmu adalah melakukan isolasi masalah dan debugging ekstrem.
**Execution Plan (Kerjakan Secara Bertahap):**
 1. **Isolasi Interseptor (Bypass Custom Network):**
   * Di RoCat, kita menggunakan AndroidCookieJar, CloudflareInterceptor, dan header kustom. Ada kemungkinan mekanisme ini merusak *request/response* internal WebView.
   * **Tugas:** Coba nonaktifkan sementara (bypass) kustomisasi shouldInterceptRequest atau interseptor OkHttp yang menyentuh WebView di BrowserScreen.kt. Buat WebView benar-benar berjalan "polosan" murni seperti aplikasi bawaan (mirip sweb-master yang polosan).
 2. **Perbaikan Layout Compose (AndroidView):**
   * Seringkali WebView terunduh tapi tidak digambar karena ukurannya 0.
   * **Tugas:** Di BrowserScreen.kt, pastikan AndroidView memiliki modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize() dan di dalam factory block, *instance* WebView secara eksplisit diberikan parameter layout:
     ```kotlin
     layoutParams = ViewGroup.LayoutParams(
         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT,
         ViewGroup.LayoutParams.MATCH_PARENT
     )
     
     ```
 3. **Aktifkan Inspektor Chrome (Wajib untuk SPA):**
   * Karena ini adalah aplikasi SPA (React), kita HARUS bisa melihat *Console* JavaScript-nya.
   * **Tugas:** Pastikan WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true) dijalankan secara statis.
   * Pastikan WebChromeClient meng-override onConsoleMessage dan mencetaknya ke Logcat dengan jelas (misal tag WebViewJS-Console), agar kita bisa melihat apakah React mengalami *Hydration Error* atau diblokir oleh CSP.
 4. **Verifikasi Hardware Acceleration & Konteks:**
   * Di AndroidManifest.xml sweb-master, periksa apakah ada tag khusus seperti android:hardwareAccelerated="true".
   * Pastikan WebView diinisialisasi menggunakan *Activity Context*, bukan *Application Context*.
**Memory and Constraints:**
 * Segera laporkan hasil ConsoleMessage dari JavaScript (apakah ada error merah dari React?).
 * Jangan merusak arsitektur BrowserViewModel yang sudah ada, cukup tambal bagian render AndroidView-nya.
 * Update 00_INDEX.md dengan penemuan terbaru terkait masalah rendering putih ini.
codex
Saya akan menelusuri `BrowserScreen.kt`, manifest, dan konfigurasi WebView/interseptor terlebih dahulu. ./DOCS_WEBVIEW.md:219:> **Warning:** Using `addJavascriptInterface()` lets JavaScript control your Android app. Although this can be useful, it can also be a dangerous security issue. When the HTML in the `WebView` is untrustworthy---for example, part or all of the HTML is provided by an unknown person or process---then an attacker can include HTML that executes your client-side code and possibly any code of the attacker's choosing. ./ai_memory/task_20260808_1404_tahap10_stealth_browser_and_cloudflare.md:7:- **`rocat-app/core/common/.../network/AndroidCookieJar.kt` (BARU):** OkHttp `CookieJar` berbasis `android.webkit.CookieManager` (jala mihon `AndroidCookieJar`). OkHttp + WebView berbagi cookie store persisten yang sama; semua akses diserialkan lock + `flush()` sinkron agar cookie yang baru diset langsung terlihat di request berikutnya; ada `get()/remove()/removeAll()` untuk cookie `cf_clearance`.
./ai_memory/task_20260808_1404_tahap10_stealth_browser_and_cloudflare.md:8:- **`.../network/interceptor/CloudflareInterceptor.kt` (BARU):** Deteksi challenge CF (HTTP 403/503 + header `Server: cloudflare` + `<title>Just a moment...` / elemen `challenge-error-*`), lalu headless WebView di main thread (via `Handler(mainLooper)`) menjalankan JS Turnstile; flag `@Volatile` dalam `ChallengeState`, latch sampai 30 detik, cek `cf_clearance` baru di `AndroidCookieJar`, retry `chain.proceed(request)`, dan WebView SELALU `destroy()` (anti-memory-leak).

./ai_memory/00_INDEX.md:8:- **Tahap 30 SELESAI** (2026-08-17): Perbaikan Interaksi Event DOM (SPA) pada Headless WebView — **klik native touch**. Analisis akar: `el.click()`/`dispatchEvent` menghasilkan event **untrusted** (`isTrusted=false`) yang diabaikan SPA (React/Vue event delegation) & anti-bot (CapCut/hCaptcha/Cloudflare). **(30.1)** `HeadlessWebViewManager.click(selector)` di-upgrade: `measureIfNeeded` (layout WebView headless ke viewport default 1366×768), `elementBounds` (`getBoundingClientRect` + `scrollIntoView`), `dispatchNativeTap` (**`MotionEvent.ACTION_DOWN`→gap→`ACTION_UP`** via `WebView.dispatchTouchEvent`, source `SOURCE_TOUCHSCREEN`, di pusat elemen → halaman melihat `isTrusted=true` touch/pointer/mouse/click), fallback `clickViaJs` (urutan pointer/mouse lama) bila elemen tak ditemukan / tap ditolak; semua helper anti-crash (`catch Throwable`, `runCatching`). **(30.2)** `RoCatBrowserWrapper` (JS polyfill): `Locator.prototype.click` kini memprioritaskan **`RoCatPage.click(selector)`** (bridge native) bila tersedia → `page.click` & `locator.click` memakai tap native, fallback JS hanya tanpa bridge. **(30.3)** `capcut_test.js` v4→v9 ditulis ulang: hapus rantai klik `evaluate` rumit + pseudo-selector `:has-text()` → tandai elemen `data-rocat-click="1"` lalu **`page.click('[data-rocat-click="1"]')`** langsung; verifikasi email/password + screenshot sebelum/sesudah tetap. **(30.4)** `DOCS_SCRIPTING.md` Bab 7 + **§7.2a Klik Native Touch (Tahap 30)** (kenapa JS click gagal + alur MotionEvent). **(30.5)** `CapCutNativeClickTest.kt` (2 test rhino: onLaunch tak sentuh browser; createAccount alur sinkron penuh → bridge `click:[data-rocat-click="1"]` → verifikasi email/password); assertion `TestBrowserlessScraperTest` diperbarui (click = bridge call). Build `:app:assembleDebug` **SUCCESS**; rhino **59 test, 54 hijau** (5 gagal = pre-existing data script hilang); fetch/RoCatDOM tak tersentuh. **(30.6) [v2 — Debug tap tidak berfungsi]**: akar = (1) koordinat DOM **CSS-px ≠ view-px** — `getBoundingClientRect` dalam CSS-px, `MotionEvent` dalam px view → wajib `scaleX=viewWidth/window.innerWidth` (poll innerWidth sampai stabil, clamp 0.1..10); tanpa ini tap meleset di layar hi-dpi/`device-width`/halaman tanpa viewport-meta (980px); (2) view headless tidak punya **fokus/window-focus** → `prepareForInteraction` (`isFocusable`+`requestFocus`+`requestFocusFromTouch`+`onWindowFocusChanged(true)`); (3) renderer belum menyerap ukuran 1366×768 pasca-`measure` → kick compositor (`draw` scratch bitmap) + `TAP_SETTLE_MS=120` sebelum DOWN + `TAP_GAP_MS=80`. `capcut_test.js` v9 + `CapCutNativeClickTest` dirombak (invoke `createAccount`, FakeBrowser branch `data-rocat-click`/`offsetParent`/`otp`). Docs §7.2a diperbarui. Build `:app:assembleDebug` SUCCESS; rhino 59 test 54 hijau (5 gagal pre-existing).

./ai_memory/00_INDEX.md:67:- Native touch click (Tahap 30): **JS `el.click()`/`dispatchEvent` menghasilkan event untrusted (`isTrusted=false`) yang diabaikan SPA & anti-bot** — `page.click(selector)`/`locator.click()` harus memakai **tap native**: `MotionEvent.obtain(downTime, downTime, ACTION_DOWN, x, y, 0)` + `ACTION_UP` via `WebView.dispatchTouchEvent` dengan `.source = InputDevice.SOURCE_TOUCHSCREEN`, di koordinat pusat elemen (`getBoundingClientRect` + `scrollIntoView({block:'center'})`). **KRITIS (v2): `getBoundingClientRect`/`window.innerWidth` = CSS pixel, `MotionEvent` = pixel view WebView** → koordinat wajib dikali rasio `scaleX = viewWidth / window.innerWidth` (dan tinggi) — poll `innerWidth/Height` 3x sampai stabil (renderer menyerap ukuran 1366×768 pasca-`measure`; 2 baca identik = stabil) dan clamp 0.1..10; tanpa ini tap meleset di layar hi-dpi/`device-width`/halaman tanpa viewport-meta (980px). View headless tak berukuran → **wajib `measure`/`layout` dulu ke viewport default (1366×768, sama dengan screenshot)** + **kick compositor** (`Bitmap` scratch + `wv.draw`) karena view detached tak punya choreographer. View detached juga tak punya **fokus/window-focus** → `prepareForInteraction`: `isEnabled/isFocusable/isFocusableInTouchMode/isClickable=true`, `setLongClickable(false)`, `requestFocus(FOCUS_DOWN)`, `requestFocusFromTouch()`, `onWindowFocusChanged(true)`. `TAP_SETTLE_MS=120` sebelum DOWN (settle `scrollIntoView`) + `TAP_GAP_MS=80` antar down/up; sleep hanya di thread skrip, UI thread bebas. Overload `MotionEvent.obtain(6 arg)` = `(long,long,int,float,float,int)` (metaState) → argumen terakhir `0` (Int), bukan `0f`. **Polyfill `RoCatBrowserWrapper` `Locator.click` harus memprioritaskan `RoCatPage.click(sel)`** (bridge native) bila ada — tanpa ini `page.click` jatuh ke JS sintetis lagi. `page.waitForSelector` di polyfill = polling `locator.exists()` via `evaluate` (BUKAN panggil `RoCatPage.waitForSelector`) — test jangan berharap call `wait:`. **Skrip jangan pakai pseudo-selector Playwright `:has-text()`** — `document.querySelector` tak bisa mem-parsingnya; pakai helper yang menemukan elemen by text + set `data-rocat-click` lalu `page.click('[data-rocat-click="1"]')`.
./ai_memory/00_INDEX.md:68:- Refactor mesin JS sweb-master (Tahap 28): **`WebSettings.setAppCacheEnabled` TIDAK ADA lagi** (compileSdk 33+; error "Unresolved reference 'appCacheEnabled'") — niat sweb-master diwarisi lewat `cacheMode = LOAD_DEFAULT`. **`SslError.SSL_HOSTMISMATCH` TIDAK ada** di SDK 35; konstanta yang benar = **`SSL_IDMISMATCH`**. **Placeholder `%1$s`/`%2$s` di strings `Strings.kt` (Kotlin) WAJIB di-escape `%1\$s`** — tanpa escape jadi "Unresolved reference 's'". `StringKey.cancel` sudah ada → jangan tambah duplikat (ambiguity). **Halaman yang "blank putih" TIDAK selalu salah konfigurasi WebView**: di engine lama (mis. WebView Chrome-113 pada image API 34 google_apis) page React modern bisa log hydration errors (#418/#423/#425) + penolakan CSP inline-script pada SETIAP konfigurasi termasuk browser sistem — buktikan dulu dengan **matriks kontrol + probe DOM** (`evaluateJavascript`: `innerHTML.length`, `scrollHeight`, `getComputedStyle(...).backgroundColor`, `document.styleSheets.length`, `arco-theme`) sebelum mengubah settings; verifikasi "bukan blank sistem" = judul ter-hydrate (`page title:` non-kosong) + tanpa `AndroidRuntime` FATAL + tanpa crash renderer. Identitas UA bersama = `NetworkHelper.DEFAULT_USER_AGENT` (Chrome/141); `getInferredUserAgent` tidak terbukti menolong di emulator dan memecah konsistensi OkHttp/WebView — jangan ganti tanpa bukti. Bukti render disimpan di `ai_memory/evidence/` (screencap + log).
./ai_memory/00_INDEX.md:69:- Emulator & verifikasi render (Tahap 27): **package di workflow harus `app.rocat.debug`** — build type debug memakai `applicationIdSuffix = ".debug"` (release = `app.rocat`); activity FQCN = `app.rocat.ui.main.MainActivity` (relative ke namespace, BUKAN applicationId). **ABI splits aktif** → output debug = `app-x86_64-debug.apk`/`app-universal-debug.apk` (tidak ada `app-debug.apk` polos); workflow memilih per-ABI lalu fallback universal. **Deep-link `app.rocat.EXTRA_URL`** dibaca MainActivity → `RoCatApp(initialUrl)` MEN-SKIP gerbang first-launch storage (browser tak butuh storage, jadi test am start langsung masuk browser), back stack diinisialisasi `[KEY_BROWSER]`, `BrowserScreen(initialUrl)` memanggil `viewModel.acceptInitialUrl(url)` (guard `initialUrlConsumed` sekali per ViewModel — pindah tab tak me-reload). **BUKTI render dari logcat `WebViewJS`**: `I/... page title: ...` non-kosong = SPA hydrate (positif); `W/... onPageFinished with blank title` = curiga blank (negatif). **Screenshot bisa dianalisis komputasional** (Pillow/ImageMagick) saat tak bisa dilihat: fraksi non-putih + palet warna — blank murni ≈99-100% putih (CapCut terverifikasi 14% non-putih + lavender `(243,237,247)` + teks `(73,69,79)` = TERENDER). **`onRenderProcessGone` recovery**: gejala blank-putih klasik = renderer crash → `view.destroy()` + `webViewEpoch += 1`; **Compose UI 1.7 (BOM 2024.12.01) `AndroidView` TIDAK punya parameter `key`** (compile error "No parameter with name 'key'") → bungkus dengan **`androidx.compose.runtime.key(webViewEpoch) { AndroidView(...) }`** (kunci slot Compose memaksa factory ulang). `onRenderProcessGone` = API 26+ (minSdk 26 aman). ./ai_memory/00_INDEX.md:70:- WebView critical JS fix (Tahap 26, sumber = DOCS_WEBVIEW.md): **Jetpack Webkit `androidx.webkit:webkit:1.8.0` WAJIB di dependensi** (dokumen §"Work with WebView on earlier versions") — device dengan System WebView lama tidak bisa memakai kapabilitas WebView terbaru tanpa library ini, halaman SPA/heavy-JS rawan blank. **`setSupportMultipleWindows(true)` TANPA override `WebChromeClient.onCreateWindow` = perilaku paling aman** yang direkomendasikan dokumen (§"Manage windows"): popup `target="_blank"` diblokir — JANGAN tambah override `onCreateWindow` tanpa alasan keamanan. **WebChromeClient minimal = `onProgressChanged`**; agar situs modern penuh, implementasikan `onShowCustomView`/`onHideCustomView` (view HTML5 video di-`re-parent` lewat `AndroidView(factory = { video })` dalam overlay `Box` full-screen; callback `CustomViewCallback.onCustomViewHidden()` harus dipanggil saat tutup; guard anti-tumpuk bila view lama masih tampil), `onPermissionRequest(request) = request.grant(request.resources)` (default WebChromeClient = deny → situs minta izin geolocation/media menggantung), dan **`onConsoleMessage(message)`** untuk meneruskan seluruh console JS ke Logcat (tag `WebViewJS`, level ERROR/WARNING/DEBUG/INFO via `android.util.Log`, format `sourceId:lineNumber: message` → SyntaxError/Cross-Origin/CSP terdiagnosa dari `adb logcat -s WebViewJS`). **BackHandler = LIFO**: handler fullscreen harus dikomposisi SETELAH handler riwayat agar Back saat fullscreen menutup video dulu. Jangan pernah panggil `loadUrl`/`reload` dari dalam `shouldOverrideUrlLoading` (dokumen §"Handle page navigation"). Akar masalah JS blank bukan mixed-content saja — verifikasi juga dependensi Jetpack Webkit & kelengkapan WebChromeClient.
./ai_memory/00_INDEX.md:71:- Browser revamp & JS engine (Tahap 25): **WebView TIDAK boleh dimiliki ViewModel** — instansinya hidup di layer Compose (`remember { mutableStateOf<WebView?>(null) }`), ViewModel mengeluarkan perintah lewat `BrowserCommand` (sealed `Reload`/`SetDesktopMode`) pada `MutableSharedFlow<BrowserCommand>(extraBufferCapacity = 4)`; UI koleksi di `LaunchedEffect(Unit)`, `DisposableEffect` tetap `destroy()` anti-leak. **Anti double-load start page**: `AndroidView(factory)` jalan SEBELUM `LaunchedEffect` → factory mengeksekusi `loadUrl` + menandai `lastAppliedNonce = state.loadNonce`, lalu `LaunchedEffect(state.loadNonce)` melewati nonce yang sama (`if (lastAppliedNonce != state.loadNonce)`). **Pull-to-refresh**: `androidx.compose.material3.pulltorefresh.PullToRefreshBox(isRefreshing, onRefresh, state = rememberPullToRefreshState())` tersedia sejak material3 1.3.0 (BOM 2024.12.01 → 1.3.1), wajib `@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)`; `isRefreshing` ditutup dari `onPageFinished`/`onProgressChanged(>=100)`/`onReceivedError`. **Mode Desktop**: `WebViewUtil.applyDesktopMode(view, desktop, mobileUA)` swap `userAgentString` (desktop = `WebViewUtil.DESKTOP_USER_AGENT` Chrome 141 Win x64) + `useWideViewPort`/`loadWithOverviewMode` selalu `true`, lalu UI `reload()`; status di-persist ke pref `desktopMode` (SettingsRepository) dan di-restore saat factory membuat WebView. **`mixedContentMode = MIXED_CONTENT_COMPATIBILITY_MODE`** wajib di `setDefaultSettings` untuk situs campuran http/https. `stringResource()` tidak boleh dipanggil dalam callback onClick → tangkap string (mis. `linkCopiedMessage`) di body composable. `onReceivedError(view, request, error)` = overload API 23+ (minSdk 26 aman). /** Logcat tag for JavaScript console messages piped from the page (Tahap 26.4). */
private const val TAG_JS = "WebViewJS"

/**
 * Tahap 25: the modern in-app browser tab. A free address bar accepts ANY URL (or a
 * plain search query), and the page is rendered by a real WebView configured for heavy
 * JavaScript sites (SPAs, local storage, mixed content).
 *
 * Because the app's `AndroidCookieJar` is backed by the WebView [android.webkit.CookieManager],
 * every cookie set while browsing here (logins, Cloudflare `cf_clearance`, ...) is
 * automatically shared with the OkHttp stack the scraper scripts run on - so a session
 * solved in the browser is immediately available to script `fetch()` calls.
 *
 * The top bar follows Material 3: a pill-shaped address bar with SSL lock / clear-text /
 * Go, back/forward, a refresh↔stop button and a three-dot overflow menu (Desktop mode,
 * reload, copy link, open in external browser). Pull-to-refresh reloads the current
 * page, and a thin [LinearProgressIndicator] tracks [WebChromeClient] load progress.
 */
@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)
@Composable
fun BrowserScreen(initialUrl: String? = null) {
    val viewModel: BrowserViewModel = viewModel(factory = AppViewModelFactory)
    val state by viewModel.state.collectAsState()
    val context = LocalContext.current
    val clipboard = LocalClipboardManager.current

    var webView by remember { mutableStateOf<WebView?>(null) }
    // Tahap 27.4: bumped when the renderer process dies (blank-white-page symptom) so
    // AndroidView rebuilds a fresh WebView and reloads the current page.
    var webViewEpoch by remember { mutableStateOf(0) }
    var isRefreshing by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    var showOptions by remember { mutableStateOf(false) }
    // Tahap 26.2 (DOCS_WEBVIEW.md §"WebChromeClient"): the page handed a custom
    // (fullscreen HTML5 video) view to the host app via onShowCustomView. Rendered as
    // a full-screen overlay; null means nothing is fullscreen.
    var fullscreenView by remember { mutableStateOf<View?>(null) }
    var customViewCallback by remember { mutableStateOf<WebChromeClient.CustomViewCallback?>(null) }
    // Guards against re-loading the start page right after the factory has already
    // loaded it (the AndroidView factory runs before the LaunchedEffect below).
    var lastAppliedNonce by remember { mutableStateOf(-1) }

    // Captured in the composable so the non-composable callbacks below can use them.
    val linkCopiedMessage = stringResource(StringKey.linkCopied)
    val sslDialogTitle = stringResource(StringKey.insecureConnectionTitle)
    val sslDialogMessage = stringResource(StringKey.insecureConnectionMessage)
    val sslProceedLabel = stringResource(StringKey.proceed)
    val sslCancelLabel = stringResource(StringKey.cancel)

    fun navState(): BrowserViewModel.NavigationState =
        BrowserViewModel.NavigationState(
            canGoBack = webView?.canGoBack() ?: false,
            canGoForward = webView?.canGoForward() ?: false,
        )

    val webViewClient = remember(viewModel) {
        object : WebViewClient() {
            override fun onPageStarted(view: WebView?, url: String?, favicon: Bitmap?) {
                viewModel.onPageStarted(url, navState())
            }

            override fun onPageFinished(view: WebView?, url: String?) {
                viewModel.onPageFinished(url, navState())
                isRefreshing = false
                // Tahap 27.4: flag pages that "finish" without a title — a strong
                // signal the SPA did not hydrate and the render came out blank, so the
                // emulator CI job can see it from `adb logcat -s WebViewJS`.
                if (view?.title.isNullOrBlank()) {
                    Log.w(TAG_JS, "onPageFinished with blank title for $url — page may be blank / JS not hydrated")
                }
            }

            // Tahap 28.2 (sweb-master `shouldOverrideUrlLoading`): `intent://` links
            // (Play Store / app deep links) fall back to their `browser_fallback_url`
            // so the in-app browser never dead-ends on a scheme it cannot load.
            override fun shouldOverrideUrlLoading(view: WebView?, url: String?): Boolean {
                if (url?.startsWith("intent://") == true) {
                    val fallbackMarker = ";S.browser_fallback_url="
                    val start = url.indexOf(fallbackMarker)
                    if (start != -1) {
                        val valueStart = start + fallbackMarker.length
                        val end = url.indexOf(';', valueStart)
                        if (end != -1 && end != valueStart) {
                            val fallback = Uri.decode(url.substring(valueStart, end))
                            view?.loadUrl(fallback)
                            return true
                        }
                    }
                }
                return false
            }

            // Tahap 28.2 (sweb-master `onReceivedSslError`): by default WebView CANCELS
            // the load on any SSL error → blank white page. Like the reference browser,

/bin/bash -lc "sed -n '180,470p' app/src/main/java/app/rocat/ui/browser/BrowserScreen.kt" in /home/runner/work/rocat-app/rocat-app

            // the load on any SSL error → blank white page. Like the reference browser,
            // surface a dialog so the user can explicitly proceed (mirroring
            // sweb-master's "Proceed" / "Cancel" flow).
            override fun onReceivedSslError(view: WebView?, handler: SslErrorHandler?, error: android.net.http.SslError?) {
                if (handler == null || error == null) return
                val errorDescription = error.primaryError.let { code ->
                    when (code) {
                        android.net.http.SslError.SSL_NOTYETVALID -> "Not yet valid"
                        android.net.http.SslError.SSL_EXPIRED -> "Expired"
                        android.net.http.SslError.SSL_IDMISMATCH -> "Hostname mismatch"
                        android.net.http.SslError.SSL_UNTRUSTED -> "Untrusted CA"
                        android.net.http.SslError.SSL_DATE_INVALID -> "Invalid date"
                        else -> "Error $code"
                    }
                }
                AlertDialog.Builder(context)
                    .setTitle(sslDialogTitle)
                    .setMessage(String.format(sslDialogMessage, error.url, errorDescription))
                    .setPositiveButton(sslProceedLabel) { _, _ -> handler.proceed() }
                    .setNegativeButton(sslCancelLabel) { _, _ -> handler.cancel() }
                    .show()
            }

            // Tahap 27.4: the classic "blank white screen" root cause on modern sites —
            // the renderer process crashed or was killed. Destroy the dead WebView and
            // bump the epoch so AndroidView rebuilds it fresh and reloads the URL.
            override fun onRenderProcessGone(view: WebView?, detail: RenderProcessGoneDetail?): Boolean {
                val crashed = detail?.didCrash() != false
                if (crashed) {
                    Log.e(TAG_JS, "renderer process gone (didCrash=$crashed) — rebuilding WebView")
                    isRefreshing = false
                    runCatching { view?.destroy() }
                    webViewEpoch += 1
                }
                return true
            }

            override fun onReceivedError(
                view: WebView?,
                request: WebResourceRequest?,
                error: WebResourceError?,
            ) {
                viewModel.refreshNavState(navState())
                isRefreshing = false
            }
        }
    }

    val chromeClient = remember(viewModel) {
        object : WebChromeClient() {
            override fun onProgressChanged(view: WebView?, newProgress: Int) {
                viewModel.onProgressChanged(newProgress)
                if (newProgress >= 100) isRefreshing = false
            }

            // Tahap 26.4: pipe every JS console.log / warning / error straight to Logcat
            // under a dedicated tag so a failing page (SyntaxError, cross-origin, CSP,
            // ...) can be diagnosed from `adb logcat -s WebViewJS`.
            override fun onConsoleMessage(message: ConsoleMessage?): Boolean {
                if (message == null) return false
                val detail =
                    "${message.sourceId()}:${message.lineNumber()}: ${message.message()}"
                when (message.messageLevel()) {
                    ConsoleMessage.MessageLevel.ERROR -> Log.e(TAG_JS, detail)
                    ConsoleMessage.MessageLevel.WARNING -> Log.w(TAG_JS, detail)
                    ConsoleMessage.MessageLevel.DEBUG -> Log.d(TAG_JS, detail)
                    else -> Log.i(TAG_JS, detail)
                }
                return true
            }

            // Tahap 27.4: log the page title to the WebViewJS tag — positive evidence
            // in the emulator CI job that the SPA actually hydrated and rendered.
            override fun onReceivedTitle(view: WebView?, title: String?) {
                if (!title.isNullOrBlank()) Log.i(TAG_JS, "page title: $title")
            }

            // Tahap 26.2 (DOCS_WEBVIEW.md §"WebChromeClient"): grant permission
            // requests (geolocation / media / protected media) so modern sites that
            // prompt at runtime never leave the page stuck waiting for an answer.
            override fun onPermissionRequest(request: PermissionRequest?) {
                request?.grant(request.resources)
            }

            // Tahap 26.2 (DOCS_WEBVIEW.md §"WebChromeClient"): fullscreen support for
            // HTML5 video — the page hands us a custom view to show edge-to-edge.
            override fun onShowCustomView(view: View?, callback: WebChromeClient.CustomViewCallback?) {
                if (fullscreenView != null) {
                    callback?.onCustomViewHidden()
                    return
                }
                fullscreenView = view
                customViewCallback = callback
            }

            override fun onHideCustomView() {
                customViewCallback?.onCustomViewHidden()
                customViewCallback = null
                fullscreenView = null
            }
        }
    }

    fun closeFullscreen() {
        customViewCallback?.onCustomViewHidden()
        customViewCallback = null
        fullscreenView = null
    }

    // Tahap 27.2: a deep link (app.rocat.EXTRA_URL) loads the URL into the browser
    // without any taps. The ViewModel consumes it once, so returning to this tab after
    // browsing elsewhere does not force the same page again.
    LaunchedEffect(initialUrl) {
        initialUrl?.let(viewModel::acceptInitialUrl)
    }

    // Navigation requests (address bar / search) arrive through the load nonce.
    LaunchedEffect(state.loadNonce) {
        if (lastAppliedNonce != state.loadNonce) {
            lastAppliedNonce = state.loadNonce
            webView?.loadUrl(state.currentUrl)
        }
    }

    // Commands that must touch the live WebView (desktop-mode switch, reload).
    LaunchedEffect(Unit) {
        viewModel.commands.collect { command ->
            val view = webView ?: return@collect
            when (command) {
                is BrowserCommand.Reload -> view.reload()
                is BrowserCommand.SetDesktopMode -> {
                    WebViewUtil.applyDesktopMode(view, command.enabled)
                    view.reload()
                }
            }
        }
    }

    // System back = go back in browser history when possible, otherwise fall through to
    // the navigation stack handled by RoCatNav.
    BackHandler(enabled = state.canGoBack) { webView?.goBack() }

    // Tahap 26.2: while a fullscreen (HTML5 video) view is on screen, Back exits it
    // first (this handler is composed after the history one, so it wins while enabled).
    BackHandler(enabled = fullscreenView != null) { closeFullscreen() }

    // Always tear the WebView down when the tab leaves the composition (no leaked views).
    DisposableEffect(Unit) {
        onDispose {
            webView?.stopLoading()
            runCatching { webView?.destroy() }
            webView = null
        }
    }

    val pullRefreshState = rememberPullToRefreshState()
    val isSecure = Uri.parse(state.currentUrl).scheme == "https"

    Box(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
        Column(modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize()) {
            // Material 3 top bar: back / forward, pill address bar, refresh↔stop, overflow.
            Row(
                verticalAlignment = Alignment.CenterVertically,
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxWidth()
                    .padding(horizontal = 4.dp, vertical = 6.dp),
            ) {
                IconButton(onClick = { webView?.goBack() }, enabled = state.canGoBack) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowBack,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(StringKey.back),
                    )
                }
                IconButton(onClick = { webView?.goForward() }, enabled = state.canGoForward) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.AutoMirrored.Filled.ArrowForward,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(StringKey.forward),
                    )
                }
                AddressBar(
                    value = state.urlInput,
                    isSecure = isSecure,
                    onValueChange = viewModel::onUrlInputChange,
                    onGo = viewModel::submitUrl,
                    onClear = viewModel::clearUrlInput,
                    modifier = Modifier.weight(1f),
                )
                if (state.isLoading) {
                    IconButton(onClick = { webView?.stopLoading() }) {
                        Icon(Icons.Filled.Close, contentDescription = stringResource(StringKey.stop))
                    }
                } else {
                    IconButton(onClick = viewModel::reload) {
                        Icon(
                            Icons.Filled.Refresh,
                            contentDescription = stringResource(StringKey.refresh),
                        )
                    }
                }
                IconButton(onClick = { showOptions = true }) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.Filled.MoreVert,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(StringKey.moreOptions),
                    )
                }
            }

            // Thin, animated progress bar wired straight to WebChromeClient.onProgressChanged.
            if (state.isLoading) {
                LinearProgressIndicator(
                    progress = { state.progress / 100f },
                    modifier = Modifier.fillMaxWidth(),
                )
            }

            PullToRefreshBox(
                isRefreshing = isRefreshing,
                onRefresh = {
                    isRefreshing = true
                    webView?.reload()
                },
                modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                state = pullRefreshState,
            ) {
                // The browser engine: a real WebView rendered through AndroidView.
                // `key(webViewEpoch)` lets onRenderProcessGone rebuild the WebView from
                // scratch (fresh AndroidView + factory) instead of a blank white screen.
                key(webViewEpoch) {
                    AndroidView(
                        modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize(),
                        factory = { ctx ->
                            WebView(ctx).also { view ->
                                // Tahap 32: use the installed WebView's native mobile UA,
                                // matching sweb-master and avoiding a synthetic Chrome
                                // version that can trip anti-bot UA consistency checks.
                                WebViewUtil.setDefaultSettings(view)
                                if (state.desktopMode) {
                                    WebViewUtil.applyDesktopMode(view, true)
                                }
                                view.webViewClient = webViewClient
                                view.webChromeClient = chromeClient
                                // Match sweb-master's download hook. Keep the WebView
                                // session/cookies intact and hand binary downloads to the
                                // platform handler instead of silently leaving the page.
                                view.setDownloadListener { url, _, _, mimeType, _ ->
                                    runCatching {
                                        context.startActivity(
                                            Intent(Intent.ACTION_VIEW)
                                                .setDataAndType(Uri.parse(url), mimeType ?: "*/*")
                                                .addFlags(Intent.FLAG_ACTIVITY_NEW_TASK),
                                        )
                                    }.onFailure {
                                        Toast.makeText(context, "Unable to open download", Toast.LENGTH_SHORT).show()
                                    }
                                }
                                webView = view
                                lastAppliedNonce = state.loadNonce
                                viewModel.refreshNavState(navState())
                                view.loadUrl(state.currentUrl)
                            }
                        },
                        update = { },
                    )
                }
            }
        }

        // Tahap 26.2: fullscreen HTML5 video overlay — the view the page requested via
        // WebChromeClient.onShowCustomView, shown edge-to-edge with a close button.
        fullscreenView?.let { video ->
            Box(
                modifier = Modifier
                    .fillMaxSize()
                    .background(Color.Black),
            ) {
                AndroidView(factory = { video }, modifier = Modifier.fillMaxSize())
                IconButton(
                    onClick = ::closeFullscreen,
                    modifier = Modifier
                        .align(Alignment.TopEnd)
                        .padding(12.dp),
                ) {
                    Icon(
                        Icons.Filled.Close,
                        contentDescription = stringResource(StringKey.closeFullscreen),
                        tint = Color.White,
                    )
                }
            }
        }
    }

exec

## Catatan Teknis Penting
- Tahap 32 (2026-08-18): audit `sweb-master` memperbaiki `setAcceptThirdPartyCookies(webView, true)` (sebelumnya getter saja), mengaktifkan `javaScriptCanOpenWindowsAutomatically`, membiarkan UA mobile bawaan WebView seperti sweb-master, dan menambah `DownloadListener`; ad-blocker sengaja tidak diimpor karena berisiko memblokir bundle SPA. Detail: `task_20260818_1200_tahap32_webview_sweb_integration.md`.
- Native touch click (Tahap 30): **JS `el.click()`/`dispatchEvent` menghasilkan event untrusted (`isTrusted=false`) yang diabaikan SPA & anti-bot** — `page.click(selector)`/`locator.click()` harus memakai **tap native**: `MotionEvent.obtain(downTime, downTime, ACTION_DOWN, x, y, 0)` + `ACTION_UP` via `WebView.dispatchTouchEvent` dengan `.source = InputDevice.SOURCE_TOUCHSCREEN`, di koordinat pusat elemen (`getBoundingClientRect` + `scrollIntoView({block:'center'})`). **KRITIS (v2): `getBoundingClientRect`/`window.innerWidth` = CSS pixel, `MotionEvent` = pixel view WebView** → koordinat wajib dikali rasio `scaleX = viewWidth / window.innerWidth` (dan tinggi) — poll `innerWidth/Height` 3x sampai stabil (renderer menyerap ukuran 1366×768 pasca-`measure`; 2 baca identik = stabil) dan clamp 0.1..10; tanpa ini tap meleset di layar hi-dpi/`device-width`/halaman tanpa viewport-meta (980px). View headless tak berukuran → **wajib `measure`/`layout` dulu ke viewport default (1366×768, sama dengan screenshot)** + **kick compositor** (`Bitmap` scratch + `wv.draw`) karena view detached tak punya choreographer. View detached juga tak punya **fokus/window-focus** → `prepareForInteraction`: `isEnabled/isFocusable/isFocusableInTouchMode/isClickable=true`, `setLongClickable(false)`, `requestFocus(FOCUS_DOWN)`, `requestFocusFromTouch()`, `onWindowFocusChanged(true)`. `TAP_SETTLE_MS=120` sebelum DOWN (settle `scrollIntoView`) + `TAP_GAP_MS=80` antar down/up; sleep hanya di thread skrip, UI thread bebas. Overload `MotionEvent.obtain(6 arg)` = `(long,long,int,float,float,int)` (metaState) → argumen terakhir `0` (Int), bukan `0f`. **Polyfill `RoCatBrowserWrapper` `Locator.click` harus memprioritaskan `RoCatPage.click(sel)`** (bridge native) bila ada — tanpa ini `page.click` jatuh ke JS sintetis lagi. `page.waitForSelector` di polyfill = polling `locator.exists()` via `evaluate` (BUKAN panggil `RoCatPage.waitForSelector`) — test jangan berharap call `wait:`. **Skrip jangan pakai pseudo-selector Playwright `:has-text()`** — `document.querySelector` tak bisa mem-parsingnya; pakai helper yang menemukan elemen by text + set `data-rocat-click` lalu `page.click('[data-rocat-click="1"]')`.
- Refactor mesin JS sweb-master (Tahap 28): **`WebSettings.setAppCacheEnabled` TIDAK ADA lagi** (compileSdk 33+; error "Unresolved reference 'appCacheEnabled'") — niat sweb-master diwarisi lewat `cacheMode = LOAD_DEFAULT`. **`SslError.SSL_HOSTMISMATCH` TIDAK ada** di SDK 35; konstanta yang benar = **`SSL_IDMISMATCH`**. **Placeholder `%1$s`/`%2$s` di strings `Strings.kt` (Kotlin) WAJIB di-escape `%1\$s`** — tanpa escape jadi "Unresolved reference 's'". `StringKey.cancel` sudah ada → jangan tambah duplikat (ambiguity). **Halaman yang "blank putih" TIDAK selalu salah konfigurasi WebView**: di engine lama (mis. WebView Chrome-113 pada image API 34 google_apis) page React modern bisa log hydration errors (#418/#423/#425) + penolakan CSP inline-script pada SETIAP konfigurasi termasuk browser sistem — buktikan dulu dengan **matriks kontrol + probe DOM** (`evaluateJavascript`: `innerHTML.length`, `scrollHeight`, `getComputedStyle(...).backgroundColor`, `document.styleSheets.length`, `arco-theme`) sebelum mengubah settings; verifikasi "bukan blank sistem" = judul ter-hydrate (`page title:` non-kosong) + tanpa `AndroidRuntime` FATAL + tanpa crash renderer. Identitas UA bersama = `NetworkHelper.DEFAULT_USER_AGENT` (Chrome/141); `getInferredUserAgent` tidak terbukti menolong di emulator dan memecah konsistensi OkHttp/WebView — jangan ganti tanpa bukti. Bukti render disimpan di `ai_memory/evidence/` (screencap + log).
- Emulator & verifikasi render (Tahap 27): **package di workflow harus `app.rocat.debug`** — build type debug memakai `applicationIdSuffix = ".debug"` (release = `app.rocat`); activity FQCN = `app.rocat.ui.main.MainActivity` (relative ke namespace, BUKAN applicationId). **ABI splits aktif** → output debug = `app-x86_64-debug.apk`/`app-universal-debug.apk` (tidak ada `app-debug.apk` polos); workflow memilih per-ABI lalu fallback universal. **Deep-link `app.rocat.EXTRA_URL`** dibaca MainActivity → `RoCatApp(initialUrl)` MEN-SKIP gerbang first-launch storage (browser tak butuh storage, jadi test am start langsung masuk browser), back stack diinisialisasi `[KEY_BROWSER]`, `BrowserScreen(initialUrl)` memanggil `viewModel.acceptInitialUrl(url)` (guard `initialUrlConsumed` sekali per ViewModel — pindah tab tak me-reload). **BUKTI render dari logcat `WebViewJS`**: `I/... page title: ...` non-kosong = SPA hydrate (positif); `W/... onPageFinished with blank title` = curiga blank (negatif). **Screenshot bisa dianalisis komputasional** (Pillow/ImageMagick) saat tak bisa dilihat: fraksi non-putih + palet warna — blank murni ≈99-100% putih (CapCut terverifikasi 14% non-putih + lavender `(243,237,247)` + teks `(73,69,79)` = TERENDER). **`onRenderProcessGone` recovery**: gejala blank-putih klasik = renderer crash → `view.destroy()` + `webViewEpoch += 1`; **Compose UI 1.7 (BOM 2024.12.01) `AndroidView` TIDAK punya parameter `key`** (compile error "No parameter with name 'key'") → bungkus dengan **`androidx.compose.runtime.key(webViewEpoch) { AndroidView(...) }`** (kunci slot Compose memaksa factory ulang). `onRenderProcessGone` = API 26+ (minSdk 26 aman). Runner: emulator perlu KVM (`sudo chmod 666 /dev/kvm` bila user bukan grup kvm); `sdkmanager "emulator" "system-images;android-34;google_apis;x86_64"`. Perbaikan pencegahan web modern: `mediaPlaybackRequiresUserGesture=false`, `CookieManager.setAcceptCookie(true)`, `WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(BuildConfig.DEBUG)`. Kebijakan Tahap 26 dipertahankan: `setSupportMultipleWindows(true)` TANPA `onCreateWindow`.
- WebView critical JS fix (Tahap 26, sumber = DOCS_WEBVIEW.md): **Jetpack Webkit `androidx.webkit:webkit:1.8.0` WAJIB di dependensi** (dokumen §"Work with WebView on earlier versions") — device dengan System WebView lama tidak bisa memakai kapabilitas WebView terbaru tanpa library ini, halaman SPA/heavy-JS rawan blank. **`setSupportMultipleWindows(true)` TANPA override `WebChromeClient.onCreateWindow` = perilaku paling aman** yang direkomendasikan dokumen (§"Manage windows"): popup `target="_blank"` diblokir — JANGAN tambah override `onCreateWindow` tanpa alasan keamanan. **WebChromeClient minimal = `onProgressChanged`**; agar situs modern penuh, implementasikan `onShowCustomView`/`onHideCustomView` (view HTML5 video di-`re-parent` lewat `AndroidView(factory = { video })` dalam overlay `Box` full-screen; callback `CustomViewCallback.onCustomViewHidden()` harus dipanggil saat tutup; guard anti-tumpuk bila view lama masih tampil), `onPermissionRequest(request) = request.grant(request.resources)` (default WebChromeClient = deny → situs minta izin geolocation/media menggantung), dan **`onConsoleMessage(message)`** untuk meneruskan seluruh console JS ke Logcat (tag `WebViewJS`, level ERROR/WARNING/DEBUG/INFO via `android.util.Log`, format `sourceId:lineNumber: message` → SyntaxError/Cross-Origin/CSP terdiagnosa dari `adb logcat -s WebViewJS`). **BackHandler = LIFO**: handler fullscreen harus dikomposisi SETELAH handler riwayat agar Back saat fullscreen menutup video dulu. Jangan pernah panggil `loadUrl`/`reload` dari dalam `shouldOverrideUrlLoading` (dokumen §"Handle page navigation"). Akar masalah JS blank bukan mixed-content saja — verifikasi juga dependensi Jetpack Webkit & kelengkapan WebChromeClient.
- Browser revamp & JS engine (Tahap 25): **WebView TIDAK boleh dimiliki ViewModel** — instansinya hidup di layer Compose (`remember { mutableStateOf<WebView?>(null) }`), ViewModel mengeluarkan perintah lewat `BrowserCommand` (sealed `Reload`/`SetDesktopMode`) pada `MutableSharedFlow<BrowserCommand>(extraBufferCapacity = 4)`; UI koleksi di `LaunchedEffect(Unit)`, `DisposableEffect` tetap `destroy()` anti-leak. **Anti double-load start page**: `AndroidView(factory)` jalan SEBELUM `LaunchedEffect` → factory mengeksekusi `loadUrl` + menandai `lastAppliedNonce = state.loadNonce`, lalu `LaunchedEffect(state.loadNonce)` melewati nonce yang sama (`if (lastAppliedNonce != state.loadNonce)`). **Pull-to-refresh**: `androidx.compose.material3.pulltorefresh.PullToRefreshBox(isRefreshing, onRefresh, state = rememberPullToRefreshState())` tersedia sejak material3 1.3.0 (BOM 2024.12.01 → 1.3.1), wajib `@OptIn(ExperimentalMaterial3Api::class)`; `isRefreshing` ditutup dari `onPageFinished`/`onProgressChanged(>=100)`/`onReceivedError`. **Mode Desktop**: `WebViewUtil.applyDesktopMode(view, desktop, mobileUA)` swap `userAgentString` (desktop = `WebViewUtil.DESKTOP_USER_AGENT` Chrome 141 Win x64) + `useWideViewPort`/`loadWithOverviewMode` selalu `true`, lalu UI `reload()`; status di-persist ke pref `desktopMode` (SettingsRepository) dan di-restore saat factory membuat WebView. **`mixedContentMode = MIXED_CONTENT_COMPATIBILITY_MODE`** wajib di `setDefaultSettings` untuk situs campuran http/https. `stringResource()` tidak boleh dipanggil dalam callback onClick → tangkap string (mis. `linkCopiedMessage`) di body composable. `onReceivedError(view, request, error)` = overload API 23+ (minSdk 26 aman). Normalisasi URL (inject `https://`, prefix `www.`, fallback `https://www.google.com/search?q=`) dipindah ke `BrowserViewModel.normalizeUrl` (fungsi private file).
- Media headers (Tahap 24): **Coil 3 TIDAK punya `ImageRequest.Builder.addHeader/addHeaders`** — header HTTP dimuat lewat `coil3.network.NetworkHeaders` + extension `coil3.network.httpHeaders` (`import coil3.network.NetworkHeaders; import coil3.network.httpHeaders`): `ImageRequest.Builder(context).data(url).httpHeaders(NetworkHeaders.Builder().apply { set(k,v) }.build())`. Video/audio pakai Media3 `DefaultHttpDataSource.Factory().setDefaultRequestProperties(headers)` (berlaku utk request playlist + segment HLS). **`java.net.URI` menolak host ber-titik-awal** (`https://.example.org` → `getHost() == null`) — `baseUrlFromMatch` perlu retry `replace("://.", "://")` setelah strip `*` supaya `https://*.example.org/*` → `https://example.org`. **`emptyMap()` tanpa argumen di `assertEquals(Object,Object)` gagal infer tipe** → gunakan `emptyMap<String, String>()`. Test recorder menyisipkan `:${headers.toSortedMap()}`; assertion `endsWith(":true:true")` yang lama otomatis menjadi `endsWith(":true:true:{}")`. `contentOrNull` adalah extension `kotlinx.serialization.json` → wajib `import kotlinx.serialization.json.contentOrNull`. UI: `SwipeToDismissBox` (`material3.rememberSwipeToDismissBoxState`) dengan `confirmValueChange` return `false` membuat item **snap-back** (tidak benar-benar ter-dismiss) — cocok utk aksi yang butuh konfirmasi (delete) atau navigasi (edit); `dismissState.dismissDirection` untuk warna/ikon background. `Modifier.animateItem()` (`foundation.lazy.LazyItemScope`, `@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi)`) = anti-flicker idiomatis untuk clear/redraw LazyColumn (bukan Crossfade ber-state shared yang tak bisa menampilkan konten lama).
- Simplification API & Template Cards (Tahap 22.2): **`RoCatCoreWrapper.js` di-inject di `RhinoScriptEngine.createScope`** dengan `cx.evaluateString(scope, RO_CAT_CORE_WRAPPER_JS, "RoCatCore.js", 1, null)` SETELAH `__rocat_sandbox__`/`RoCatUI`/`RoCatDOM`/`document` dan SEBELUM kode user — wrapper harus ditulis ES5 Rhino-1.7.15-safe (tanpa `async`/`class`/spread/optional-chaining) dan tiap dispatch `renderOne` dibungkus `try/catch` + guard `hasUI()` (`typeof RoCatUI !== "undefined"`). `RoCat.render` memetakan `type` → panggilan `RoCatUI.add*` dengan `pick()`/`pickBool()`; `type:"json"` men-serialize objek non-string via `JSON.stringify`; `type:"badges"` menerima array JS ATAU string JSON. **`fetch().json()` TIDAK boleh langsung `JsonParser.parseValue`**: Rhino melempar *checked* `ParseException` yang tak bisa ditangkap `try/catch` JS → selalu lewat `throwJsError(cx, scope, msg)` (`cx.newObject(scope, "Error", arrayOf(message))` + `throw JavaScriptException(error, null, 0)`). `RoCat.fetchJson` memilih `safeParseJson(res.body)` (pakai `JSON.parse` JS yang SyntaxError-nya catchable) bukan `res.json()`. `ScriptUiBridge` method baru wajib ber-**default no-op** (bukan abstract) supaya `RecordingUiBridge` lama & implementasi test lain tetap valid. `argJson(args, i)` di `RoCatUiBridge`: argumen `Scriptable` (objek/array JS) → `NativeJSON.stringify`, `ConsString`/String → `Context.toString`, `Undefined` → default "". UI card `AlertBannerCard` normalisasi level via `AlertType.from(...)` fallback info (bridge meneruskan apa adanya, tak pernah throw).
- Native Base64 & network settings (Tahap 20): **default method `ScriptUiBridge.decodeBase64` TIDAK boleh pakai `android.util.Base64`** — unit test JVM rhino yang memanggilnya kena "Method not mocked"; gunakan `java.util.Base64` (safe di JVM + Android API26+), override native `android.util.Base64` hanya di app (`ScriptCanvasViewModel`). Fungsi bridge yang mengembalikan nilai (decodeBase64) wajib pakai `runSafeValue(default)` (bukan `runSafe` yang mengembalikan `Unit`) agar JS menerima string. `DnsMode` (SYSTEM/CLOUDFLARE/GOOGLE/QUAD9/CUSTOM) + `DnsProviders.dohUrl()` + `DoHResolver` (impl `okhttp3.Dns`, klien DoH sendiri `.dns(Dns.SYSTEM)` anti-rekursi, fallback `Dns.SYSTEM` bila DoH gagal — tanpa dependensi `okhttp3-dnsoverhttps`). **`NetworkHelper.client` kini fungsi `client()`** yang dibangun **lazily** hanya saat fingerprint `"$ua|$mode|$customUrl"` berubah (pemanggil lama: MediaDownloader, ScriptSourceFetcher, ScriptManager di-update). `SettingsRepository` simpan `user_agent`/`dns_mode`/`custom_dns_url` ke pref; `AppModule` buat SettingsRepository **sebelum** NetworkHelper (seed config awal dari pref); `ScriptManager.engine()/environment()/createEnvironment()` kini FUNGSI yang memanggil `refresh()` + membandingkan `NetworkHelper.fingerprint()` → engine/env dibangun ulang saat config berubah.
- Anichin scraper end-to-end (Tahap 19): **`fetch()` skrip TIDAK membaca `DefaultScriptEnvironment.fetchImpl`** — bridge Rhino selalu lewat OkHttp client. Untuk test hermetic pakai `okhttp3.Interceptor` yang me-intercept host target dan membalas HTML canned (`Response.Builder` + `toResponseBody("text/html; charset=utf-8".toMediaType())`; okhttp 4.12). `scrape_anichin.js`: `pickBestVariant` buang baris `#EXT-X-STREAM-INF` yang tidak diikuti URI (master anichin.stream berisi varian 1080p malformed) → kirim URL varian valid ke `RoCatUI.addVideo(url, title, true, true)`; `decodeBase64` coba `android.util.Base64` lalu fallback decoder murni JS (pad otomatis); `readInput` dukung map polos & `{ get }`; skrip memakai hanya sintaks Rhino 1.7.15 aman (sync, tanpa async/spread/optional-chaining/class).
- Media Template, Downloader & HLS (Tahap 18): **Media3 1.4.1** (`media3-exoplayer`, `media3-exoplayer-hls`, `media3-ui`) aman untuk R8 preview tanpa rule tambahan. `RocatVideoPlayer` memakai `ExoPlayer.Builder` + `DefaultHttpDataSource.Factory().setAllowCrossProtocolRedirects(true).setUserAgent(DEFAULT_USER_AGENT)`; HLS dideteksi dari flag `isStreamHls` ATAU URL `.m3u8`/`hls://` → `HlsMediaSource.Factory`, selainnya `ProgressiveMediaSource.Factory`. **Full screen WAJIB `Dialog(usePlatformDefaultWidth=false)`** untuk menembus Scaffold/SystemBar; window dialog diambil via `(LocalView.current.parent as? DialogWindowProvider)?.window` (sudah STABIL di Compose 1.7 — `ExperimentalComposeUiApi` justru unresolved, jangan pakai `@OptIn`). Orientasi `SCREEN_ORIENTATION_SENSOR_LANDSCAPE` masuk / `PORTRAIT` keluar + `WindowInsetsControllerCompat` hide/show system bars; **`MainActivity` butuh `android:configChanges="orientation|screenSize|screenLayout|keyboardHidden|smallestScreenSize|uiMode"`** agar rotasi tidak recreate activity (kalau tidak, Dialog & state full screen hilang). Player SAMA di-share inline↔fullscreen; inline `PlayerView` di-detach (`update { player = null }`) saat dialog aktif. Download: `MediaDownloader` (OkHttp `NetworkHelper.client` + `StorageManager.saveFileToScrapeFolder`) — `CloudflareInterceptor` aman dari thread IO; status card via `DownloadStatus` (Idle/Downloading/Done/Failed) + `CircularProgressIndicator(progress = { fraction })` (overload lambda di material3 1.3). Bridge baru `RoCatUI.addImage`/`addVideo` (ScriptUiBridge) + helper Rhino `argBoolean(args,index,default)`; `ScriptUIComponent.Thumbnail` diganti `Image` (backward-compat `thumbnailPreview` tetap jalan).
- First-launch, storage fisik skrip & kategori (Tahap 17): **Gate first-launch harus reaktif** — `SettingsRepository.storageUri` sekarang `StateFlow<Uri?>` (setter `setStorageUri()`), `StorageManager.isConfigured` = `StateFlow<Boolean>` (`.map { it != null }` + `.stateIn(scope, SharingStarted.Eagerly, ...)`); akses nilai via `.value` (SettingsViewModel) atau `collectAsState()` (RoCatApp). Skrip impor ditulis sebagai file fisik `[Utama]/Scripts/[id]/<id>.js` via `StorageManager.saveFileToScriptFolder` (DocumentFile.createFile + `openOutputStream`, mime `application/javascript`); `ImportScriptViewModel.persistToStorage()` best-effort (wrap `runCatching`) supaya import tak gagal walau storage belum siap; `ScriptsViewModel.delete()` panggil `deleteScriptFolder`. Long-press pakai `Modifier.combinedClickable` (butuh `@OptIn(ExperimentalFoundationApi::class)`), menu `ModalBottomSheet` (Edit → `Screen.Detail`, Hapus → `AlertDialog` + `viewModel.delete`). Kategori: parser baca `@category` (fallback `@group`); field `category` baru di `ScriptMetadata` + `Script` (default `""`) AMAN untuk decode `scripts.json` lama (kotlinx pakai default saat field hilang); `ScriptsScreen` grup via `groupBy { it.category }`, header kategori `item(key = "category:$name")` + expand state `remember { mutableStateMapOf<String, Boolean>() }` (missing = expanded), label kategori kosong = i18n `StringKey.othersCategory`. `stringResource` hanya boleh dipanggil di dalam blok `item {}` composable, bukan di level builder `forEach` LazyListScope.
- Browser Bug Storage & In-App Browser (Tahap 16): **`RoCatUI.save()`** → `ScriptUiBridge.saveFile(fileName, content, mimeType)` → `StorageManager.saveFileToScrapeFolder()` (DocumentFile.createFile + `contentResolver.openOutputStream`, overload String/byte, sanitize nama). **Jangan pakai `WebView(...).apply { }` di dalam `AndroidView(factory)` saat ada variabel local bernama sama** (`webViewClient`, `webView`, `currentUrl`) → `'val' cannot be reassigned`; gunakan `also { view -> view.webViewClient = ... }`. Interface `ScriptUiBridge` berubah → semua implementasi (termasuk unit test `RecordingUiBridge`) wajib menambah `saveFile`. Clear cookies tuntas = Room `cookieDao.deleteAll()` + `CookieManager.removeAllCookies(null)` + `flush()`; clear cache = Coil + `cacheDir` + `WebView(context).clearCache(true)` (main thread). `BrowserScreen`: WebView via `AndroidView` dipakai `WebViewUtil.setDefaultSettings` (JS/DOM/UA), `Back/Forward/Refresh/Stop` langsung ke riwayat WebView, `BackHandler(enabled=canGoBack)`, `DisposableEffect` `destroy()`; cookie login di browser otomatis dipakai mesin scraper via `AndroidCookieJar`.
- Lokalisasi & storage (Tahap 15): i18n pakai `StringKey` enum + `Strings` **open class** (bukan data class — harus bisa diturunkan oleh `EnglishStrings`/`IndonesianStrings`); `staticCompositionLocalOf<Strings>` butuh tipe eksplisit; `stringResource()` **tidak boleh** dipanggil di callback non-composable (onClick/onFailure) — tangkap string di body composable. Storage: `takePersistableUriPermission` + simpan tree URI di SharedPreferences (`SettingsRepository`); folder scrape `[Utama]/Scrapes/[id]/` via `StorageManager.createScrapeFolder()` (DocumentFile); `clearCache()` = Coil memory+`diskCache.clear()` (suspend → `Dispatchers.IO`) + `context.cacheDir`; first-launch `StorageSetupScreen` memblokir UI sampai folder dipilih. Room 2.6.1 + KSP `2.0.21-1.0.28` (cocok Kotlin 2.0.21), DAO/DB singleton via Injekt; R8 preview jalan tanpa rule tambahan.
- Production Readiness (Tahap 14): build type `preview` (`initWith(release)`, suffix `.preview`) WAJIB juga didefinisikan di setiap modul library (`core:common`, `core:viewmodel`, `domain`, `data`, `scripting:api`, `scripting:rhino`) dengan `initWith(getByName("release"))`; tanpa ini variant matching AGP gagal (app preview tak bisa konsumsi varian library). Signing: `signingConfigs.release` hanya terisi bila `keystore.properties` + keystore ada (`hasReleaseSigning`), jika tidak `release`/`preview` fallback ke `signingConfigs.debug` → build tak pernah gagal. ABI splits aktif: 4 APK per-ABI (`armeabi-v7a`/`arm64-v8a`/`x86`/`x86_64`) + `isUniversalApk = true`. ProGuard Wajib Rhino: `-dontwarn java.beans.**` + `-dontwarn org.mozilla.javascript.**` (R8 error tanpa ini karena `JavaToJSONConverters` mereferensikan `java.beans.*` yang tidak ada di Android). Firebase: plugin `google-services` & `firebase.crashlytics` dideklarasikan `apply false` di root, dan hanya di-apply + deps Firebase ditambahkan bila `app/google-services.json` ada.
- Full Script Canvas & Grid (Tahap 13): `RoCatUI` ekspansi `addGrid(columns, itemsJson, onClickFunction)`; `ScriptCanvasScreen`/`ScriptCanvasViewModel` memakai entry point `onLaunch()` (bukan `buildUI`), auto-run + session token, tombol Button mengumpulkan Input -> `Map<id,value>` via `invokeNamedFunction`; klik item Grid -> `invoke(script, fn, args = listOf(item.rawJsonPayload))` (payload JSON string yang di-`JSON.parse` lagi oleh JS); komponen `Grid`/`GridItem(title,imageUrl,rawJsonPayload)` di-parse dari JSON array via kotlinx-serialization; `GridView` = `LazyVerticalGrid` (`userScrollEnabled=false`) pakai tinggi terukur (tile 180dp) supaya bisa dimuat di dalam LazyColumn; `NavigationBar` hanya untuk Scripts/Playground (kanvas full-screen); `ScriptsScreen` menampilkan cover `script.icon` (`@icon`/`@iconURL`) via Coil `AsyncImage`, fallback ikon Extension. Contoh `CANVAS_EXAMPLE_SCRIPT` = demo Search->Grid->Detail ('Manga Scraper Mock') tersedia via tombol 'Canvas demo' di Import.
- Script-Driven UI (Tahap 12 revisi): global `RoCatUI` tersedia di Rhino scope saat `environment.ui != null` (`ScriptUiBridge`: `addInput(id,hint)`, `addButton(label,functionName)`, `thumbnailPreview(url)`, `videoPreview(url)`, `clear()`, `log(text)`); `buildUI()` dipanggil Kotlin otomatis; tombol mengumpulkan semua nilai `ScriptUIComponent.Input` → `Map<id,value>` → `ScriptEngine.invokeNamedFunction()` mengubahnya jadi 1 argumen objek JS (`inputs.video_url`); return `undefined` diflatkan jadi `""`; UI update dimarshal `Dispatchers.Main` + `uiSession` token membuang render lama. Kontrak lama `{"media_type":"image"/"video","media_url":...}` masih dirender di `CopyableResultCard`.
- Media Renderer & Smart Inputs (Tahap 12 awal): argumen Playground value-only (`testArgs: List<String>`, default `List(1){""}`, tombol "+ Add Input"); regex Public vs Private = `function\s+(?!_)([a-zA-Z$][\w$]*)\s*\(`; Coil 3.0.4 (`coil-compose` + `coil-network-okhttp`). — Digantikan oleh Playground script-driven (testArgs dihapus).
- Playground hotfix (Tahap 11.5): tidak ada lagi card "Run main"/Target URL; `testFunctionSuggestions` diisi otomatis dari `extractFunctionNames(script.source)` (Regex `function\s+([a-zA-Z_$][\w$]*)\s*\(`) saat skrip dipilih/dimuat, `testFunction` default = fungsi pertama; `testArgs` default `emptyList()` → input hanya muncul setelah tombol "+ Add Input"; display & copy di `CopyableResultCard` memakai `ResultFormatter.prettyJson` (JSON pretty-print, non-JSON fallback raw) dan teks dibungkus `SelectionContainer` agar bisa diseleksi. 
- Playground dinamis (Tahap 11): `testArgs: List<TestArg(label,value)>` (baris input Key+Value bisa ditambah/dihapus, nilai blank dilewati saat pemanggilan); `testFunction` editable + suggestion (`search/detail/main`) → `ExecuteScript.invoke(script, fn, args)`; hasil bisa disalin via `CopyableResultCard` (Copy JSON = pretty-print `Json { prettyPrint=true }` fallback ke teks asli jika bukan JSON; Copy Text = raw) dengan `LocalClipboardManager` + `Toast`.
- Stealth network (Tahap 10): `AndroidCookieJar` = OkHttp `CookieJar` ber-basis `WebView CookieManager` → cookie OkHttp & WebView identik (persisten, sync + `flush()`); `CloudflareInterceptor` bypass CF challenge via headless WebView di main thread (`Handler(mainLooper)`, latch 30s, cek cookie `cf_clearance` baru, retry, lalu `destroy()` WebView); `StealthHeadersInterceptor` default header Chromium (`Sec-CH-UA*`, `Sec-Fetch-*`, `Accept-Language`) hanya bila header belum ada; `NetworkHelper(context)` pasang cookie jar + UA + stealth headers + CF interceptor, `newScriptClient()` mewarisi semua.
- Network (Tahap 7): `network_security_config.xml` aktif (cleartext + trust system/user); `NetworkHelper.DEFAULT_USER_AGENT` = `Chrome/141.0.0.0`; ConnectionSpec `MODERN_TLS/COMPATIBLE_TLS/CLEARTEXT`; `ScriptSourceFetcher.normalizeUrl()` otomatis inject `https://` & rewrite GitHub blob→raw; `WebViewUtil.setDefaultSettings()` utk engine fallback.
- Rhino 1.7.15: support `let/const`, arrow fn, template literal, Promise, generator; TIDAK support `async/await`, spread, optional-chaining, class. Script sample harus ditulis sync (`fetch()` mengembalikan objek Response sync dengan `.text()`/`.json()`).
- Rhino di Android WAJIB mode interpretasi (`optimizationLevel = -1` di `ScriptContextFactory.makeContext()`): tanpa ini Rhino mengompilasi JS ke `.class` Java standar yang tidak bisa dimuat classloader Android → error "can't load this type of class file". Efek samping: bilangan bulat dikembalikan sbg `Double` (`42.0`) → dinormalisasi via `valueToString()`.
- `fetch()` bridge: sync, melewati `scriptFetch` (OkHttp client app), balikkan `{status,statusText,ok,url,body,headers,error,text(),json()}`. Arg URL harus dikonversi `Context.toString` (Rhino `ConsString` bukan `java.lang.String`).
- `RoCatDOM` (Tahap 8): global DOM bridge berbasis Jsoup; `parse/select/selectText/selectAttr/selectHtml/has`; elemen wrapper punya `text,html,innerHtml,attrs,attr(),has(),contains(),find(),textOf(),attrOf(),textsOf(),nextElement()` — semua balik nilai JS native.
- `ScriptEngine.invokeFunction()` (Tahap 8): evaluasi source → panggil fungsi bernama (`search`/`detail`) → hasil `NativeJSON.stringify`. Dipakai Playground "Test Execution" (Run Search/Run Detail).
- Watchdog: `ScriptContextFactory` + instruction budget 10M; timeout network di `NetworkHelper.newScriptClient()`.
- Parser metadata: blok `==UserScript==` atau fallback `// @tag`; tag `@name/@version/@description/@author/@match/@include/@icon(/iconURL)`.

codex
Temuan awal: `AndroidView` sudah memakai `fillMaxSize()` dan console sudah diteruskan, tetapi tag-nya masih `WebViewJS`, bukan tag diagnostik yang diminta. codex
Saya akan menambal hanya layer UI: menambahkan `layoutParams` MATCH_PARENT, mengaktifkan debugging Chromium secara statis, mengganti tag console menjadi `WebViewJS-Console` dengan URL/level lengkap, dan memastikan factory memakai activity context bila tersedia. Tidak ada `shouldInterceptRequest` di `BrowserScreen.kt`; karena itu bypass jaringan custom dilakukan dengan tidak memasang interseptor apa pun pada WebView, sementara `WebViewClient` tetap dipakai hanya untuk navigasi/error lifecycle. Manifest RoCat belum mendeklarasikan akselerasi, jadi saya akan menambah `android:hardwareAccelerated="true"` pada application.

codex
Ada satu detail konteks yang perlu diperketat: context dari Compose biasanya berupa wrapper bertema, bukan instance `Activity` langsung. Saya akan membuka rantai `ContextWrapper` sampai menemukan Activity dan gagal cepat bila memang tidak ada, sehingga tidak diam-diam jatuh ke application context. Setelah itu saya jalankan kompilasi Kotlin dan inspeksi diff.

+  `shouldInterceptRequest` dan tidak memasang OkHttp/interceptor custom pada WebView;
+  jalur resource dibiarkan native Chromium. `AndroidView` dan instance WebView kini
+  memakai `MATCH_PARENT` eksplisit, WebView dibuat dengan Activity context, dan manifest
+  mengaktifkan `android:hardwareAccelerated="true"`. `WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true)`
+  aktif statis; `WebChromeClient.onConsoleMessage` mencatat level, source, baris, dan pesan
+  ke Logcat tag `WebViewJS-Console`. Log console aktual belum dapat dikumpulkan tanpa
+  menjalankan APK pada emulator/device; gunakan `adb logcat -s WebViewJS-Console` untuk
+  membedakan React hydration/CSP error dari kegagalan renderer/layout.

+  mengaktifkan `android:hardwareAccelerated="true"`. `WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true)`
+  aktif statis; `WebChromeClient.onConsoleMessage` mencatat level, source, baris, dan pesan
+  ke Logcat tag `WebViewJS-Console`. Log console aktual belum dapat dikumpulkan tanpa
+  menjalankan APK pada emulator/device; gunakan `adb logcat -s WebViewJS-Console` untuk
+  membedakan React hydration/CSP error dari kegagalan renderer/layout.

codex
Perubahan Tahap 32.2 sudah diterapkan dan build berhasil.

Yang diperbaiki:

- `AndroidView` dan `WebView` kini memakai ukuran eksplisit `MATCH_PARENT`.
- WebView dibuat menggunakan `Activity context`; jika context bukan Activity, proses akan gagal jelas daripada memakai `Application context`.
- `WebView.setWebContentsDebuggingEnabled(true)` diaktifkan.
- `WebChromeClient.onConsoleMessage` mencatat level, source, nomor baris, dan pesan ke tag:

  `WebViewJS-Console`

- Tidak ada `shouldInterceptRequest` di `BrowserScreen.kt`, dan tidak ada interseptor OkHttp yang dipasang langsung ke WebView. Resource dibiarkan diproses native Chromium.
- Manifest RoCat kini menetapkan:

  `android:hardwareAccelerated="true"`

- Temuan sudah dicatat di [00_INDEX.md](/home/runner/work/rocat-app/rocat-app/ai_memory/00_INDEX.md).

Verifikasi:

```text
BUILD SUCCESSFUL
:app:compileDebugKotlin
```

Console JavaScript aktual belum dapat dikumpulkan karena membutuhkan APK berjalan di emulator/device. Gunakan:

```bash
adb logcat -s WebViewJS-Console
```

Jika halaman tetap putih, hasil log tersebut akan membedakan React hydration/CSP error dari kegagalan renderer atau layout.
